(6) ceṟippu: iruviṉaikaḷum curakkum ūṟṟiṉatu vaḻiyai aṭaittu viṭuvatu ceṟippu eṉappaṭum. uyir oru piṟappil ceyta iruviṉaikaḷai maṟupiṟappil tuyttu nīkkumpoḻutē putiya viṉaikaḷ vantu cērātapaṭi taṭuttalē ceṟippu eṉappaṭum.
(7) iṅṅaṉam iruviṉaiyiṉ ūṟṟu mēlum perukāmal taṭutta piṉpu, tuyttuk kaḻikkāmal eñci niṉṟa viṉaikaḷait tavam mutaliyavaṟṟāl nīkki viṭutal utirppu eṉappaṭum.
(8) maṉam, moḻi, mey, aimpulaṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl uṇṭākum viṉaikaḷ uyiruṭaṉ kalappatu kaṭṭu eṉappaṭum.
(9) aimpulaṉkaḷai avittu viṉaikaḷiliruntu nīṅkiya uyir kaṭaiyilā aṟivu- kāṭci- vīriyam- iṉpam ivaṟṟai aṭaintu vīṭupēṟu peṟutal vīṭu eṉappaṭum.
vīṭu peṟṟa uyirē kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu camaṇa camayak karuttu. mēlē kūṟappeṟṟa oṉpatu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyai aṟivatu naṉñāṉam eṉappaṭum; avaṟṟiṉ taṉmaiyai uṇarvatu naṟkāṭci eṉappaṭum. ivviraṇṭaiyum uḷattiṟ koṇṭu oḻukuvatē nalloḻukkam eṉappaṭum. im mūṉṟum irattiṉat tirayam eṉṟum mummaṇi eṉṟum collap paṭum. ivai mūṉṟum vīṭu pēṟṟukku iṉṟiyamaiyātavai. (mayilai cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi, camaṇamum tamiḻum, pak. 1-5)
(2) pautta camaya tattuvam
putta camayam
puttar aracakumārar; ki.mu. 6-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar; uyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpattai oḻikkat tuṟavu pūṇṭu muṉivar palaruṭaṉ ceṉṟu, tattuva vicāraṇai ceytu, iṟutiyil pōti (araca) mara niḻalil amarntu yōkattil iruntār. appōtu avarukku viḷaṅkiya uṇmaikaḷē putta camaya tattuvam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avar aṉṟu mutal puttar (ñāṉi) eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭār. pautta camayam puttar kālattil oṉṟākavē iruntatu. piṉpu, atu iraṇṭākap pirintatu. puttar kālac camayam hīṉayāṉam eṉappaṭṭatu; piṉpu tōṉṟiyatu makāyāṉam eṉappaṭṭatu.
puttar karuttukkaḷ
ulaka vāḻvu tuṉpakkaṭal- at tuṉpattait toṭarcciyāka vaḷarppatu maṟupiṟappu- makkaḷ aṭaiyattakum mēlāṉa uṟutipporuḷ piṟavāmaiyē - paṟṟaṟṟup payaṉ nōkkātu ceyyum naṟceyalkaḷē ap piṟavāmaikkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷākum; eṉpaṉa puttar kaṇṭaṟinta uṇmaikaḷ cuyanalattaik karuti muyalvatu aṟamaṉṟu-am muyaṟci ulakappaṟṟiṉai vaḷarttukkoṇṭē varuvatu. ātalāl payaṉ karutāmal piṟar poruṭṭuc ceyyum ceyalē, tuṉpantarum ulakap paṟṟiṉai aṟuppatu eṉpatu puttar koḷkai.
puttar uraitta aṟam
(1) nāṉku niṉaivukaḷ: (1) uṭal tūymaiyaṟṟatu eṉṟu niṉaittal, (2) nukarvukaḷiliruntu uṇṭākum kēṭukaḷai niṉaittal, (3) eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ nilaiyaṟṟa taṉmaiyai niṉaittal, (4) iruppukku iyalpāṉa taṉmaikaḷai niṉaittal.
(2) nāṉku naṉmuyaṟcikaḷ: (1) tīya kuṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭākāmal taṭukka muyalutal, (2) piṉṉarē uṇṭāki yirukkum tīyakuṇaṅkaḷai vilakka muyalutal, (3) muṉpu illāta naṉmaiyai uṇṭākka muyalutal, (4) muṉpu uṇṭākiyuḷḷa naṉmaiyai mikaippaṭutta muyalutal.
3. cittiyaip peṟutaṟkuriya nāṉku vaḻikaḷ: (1) iruttiyaip peṟavēṇṭum eṉṉum niṉaippu, (2) iruttiyaip peṟuvataṟkuriya muyaṟci, (3) iruttiyaip peṟuvataṟkuriya maṉanilai, (4) iruttiyaip peṟuvataṟkuriya ārāycci.
4. aivakai āṟṟalkaḷ: (1) srīttai, (2) tiṟamai, (3) niṉaivu, (4) uruvēṟṟal, (5) ūkittal.
5. uṇmai ñāṉattai uṇṭākkuvataṟkuriya karuvikaḷ: (1) tiṟamai, (2) niṉaivu, (3) uruvēṟṟal(maṉaṉam), (4) tiripiṭaka ārāycci. (cūttirappiṭakam, viṉayapiṭakam, apitarma piṭakam eṉṉum mūvakaippaṭṭa pautta ākamat tokuti)  (5) makiḻcci, (6) amaiti, (7) otta pārvai (camatiruṣṭi).
ivaṟṟiṉ vaḻiniṉṟu paṟṟai aṟukka vēṇṭum. paṟṟai aṟuppataṟku vēṟu iruvakai vaḻikaḷum uṇṭu. avai aintu pāvaṉaikaḷ, nāṉku tiyāṉaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa.
1. aintu pāvaṉaikaḷ: (1) maittirī pāvaṉai, (2) karuṇā pāvaṉai, (3) mutitapāvaṉai, (4) acupapāvaṉai; (5) upēṭcā pāvaṉai.
1. maittirīpāvaṉai: pautta tuṟavi,ellā uyirkaḷum pērācai; nōy, tuṉpam ivaṟṟiliruntu viṭupaṭṭuk kaḷippuṟṟu vāḻaṭṭum eṉṟu pāvittal.
2. karuṇāpāvaṉai: vaṟiyavar vaṟumai nīṅkic celvam peṟuka eṉṟu pāvittal.
3. mutita pāvaṉai: ovvoruvarum tattamakku amaintuḷḷa nalviṉaippayaṉai aṭaivārāka eṉṟu pāvittal. (mutitam- makiḻcci)
4. acupapāvaṉai: uṭal mikavum iḻintatu- veṟuttaṟ kuriya nāṟṟattai veḷippaṭuttuvatu eṉṟu pāvittal. (acupam- amaṅkalam)
5. upēṭcāpāvaṉai: ellā uyirkaḷaiyum oṉṟākak karututalum, viruppu veṟuppiṉṟi iruttalum ākum. (upēṭcai- utācīṉam, alaṭciyam.)
nāṉku tiyāṉaṅkaḷ
mutalām tiyāṉam: tuṟavi, kāmam pāvam ivaṟṟiliruntu taṉṉai vilakki maṉaṉattōṭum ārāycciyōṭum kūṭiyirukkumpōtu anupavikkum makiḻcci nilai.
iraṇṭām tiyāṉam: maṉaṉamum ārāycciyum illāmal avai aṭaikkappaṭṭu irukkumpōtu, amaitiyiliruntu uṇṭākum iṉpanilai.
mūṉṟām tiyāṉam: tuṟavi, kāmam mutaliyavaṟṟai oḻittu, makiḻcciyaip peṟṟu, poṟumai aṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu kūṭiya iṉpattait taṉṉuḷ anupavittal.
nāṉkām tiyāṉam: iṉpamum illāmal tuṉpamum illāmal irukkiṉṟa otta pārvaiyum aṟivaiyum koṇṭuḷḷa tūya nilai.
it tiyāṉaṅkaḷ nāṉkum camātikku utaviyāyuḷḷaṉa.
putta camayattiṉarkkup puttar, tarmam, caṅkam eṉṉum mūṉṟum ciṟantavai. ivai, mummaṇikaḷ eṉappaṭum. mēlē collappeṟṟavai pautta tarmattaic cērntavai. ivai allāmal pautta tarmattil, tāy tantaiyar- makkaḷukkuriya oḻukkaṅkaḷ, āciriyar- māṇavarkkuriya oḻukkaṅkaḷ, naṇpar oruvariṭattil oruvar naṭantukoḷḷattakum oḻukkaṅkaḷ, mutalāḷikkum toḻilāḷikkum uriya oḻukkaṅkaḷ, illaṟattārkkum tuṟavikkum uriya oḻukkaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa virivākavum teḷivākavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tuṟavikaḷ, mēlē collappeṟṟa cittikkuriya tuṟaikaḷil veṟṟi peṟṟāl, parinirvāṇam eṉṉum tuṉpamaṟṟa nilaiyai aṭaivar eṉṟu pautta nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa! (maṇimēkalai, pak.96-107)
3. atvaitam
caṅkarar
ivar, ki.pi. 8-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil malaiyāḷa māvaṭṭatti luḷḷa kālaṭi eṉṉum ūril piṟantavar. ivar caiva camayattaic cērntavar. ivaratu koḷkai atvaitam eṉappaṭum.
ulakattil kāṇappaṭum poruḷkaḷ yāvum nam pulaṉkaḷiṉuṭaiya maruṭciyiṉāl kāṇappaṭum tōṟṟaṅkaḷē allāmal uṇmaiyāṉavai alla.
nam kaṇkaḷukkut terintuḷḷa ulakattilum teriyāta ulakaṅkaḷilum paravi, uyirkkuyirāka irukkiṉṟa oṉṟē piramam eṉpatu. collaḷavu appiramam vēṟu, ulakam vēṟu eṉṟu collalām. āṉāl, ulakam piramattai ātāramākak koṇṭu ataṉkaṇṇē irukkiṉṟatu. ātalāl, ulakam, piramam ākiya iraṇṭum vēṟu alla- iraṇṭu alla- iraṇṭum aṟṟa nilai. inta iraṇṭum aṟṟa nilaiyē vaṭa moḻiyil atvaitam eṉappaṭum.
piramam ulakamākap pariṇamippatum, ulakam piramattuḷ oṭuṅkuvatum inta atvaita cittāntattiṉ viti. cātāraṇa aṟivilpiramam eṉavum, ulakam eṉavum iraṇṭākat tōṉṟiṉālum, ñāṉa neṟiyil ellām piramamākavē kāṇappaṭuvatāl, ivai parañāṉam, apara ñāṉattiṟkuriya uṭkaruviyākiya maṉattiṉkaṇ aruḷuṇarvāyt tōṉṟu kiṟatu. centāmarai cemmai + tāmarai. paṇpum poruḷum pōla iraṇṭāyum, paṇpaippōla oṉṟāyum irukkum taṉmaiyē atvaitam.
atvaitak koḷkaiyiṉpaṭi, uyir uṭaliṉum vēṟupaṭṭatu; uṇmai yāṉatu; ulakap paṟṟukkaḷiliruntu viṭupaṭṭavuṭaṉ iṟaivaṉiṭam cērvatu. maṉitaṉ aṟivu iruvakaippaṭum. avai, (1) aparañāṉam, (2) parañāṉam eṉpaṉa.
(1) kaṇ, kātu pōṉṟa poṟi, pulaṉkaḷālum, maṉam, patti, cittam, akaṅkāram mutaliya antakkaraṇaṅkaḷālum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟa inta ulakam paṟṟiya aṟivē cātāraṇa aṟivu *(aparañāṉam). inta aṟivu, meypōlat tōṉṟip poyyāki nilaiyaṟṟu oḻivatu.
(2) inta ulakiṟku ātāramāy irukkiṉṟa piramattaippaṟṟiya aṟivē mēlāṉa aṟivu (parañāṉam) anta aṟivu nilaiyāṉatu. piramattai aṟiyum vaḻi pāramārttikam eṉappaṭum. uyir mēlāṉa aṟivai aṟintatum, ataṉaip paṟṟiyirunta paṟṟukkaḷ akaṉṟu, anta nilaiyil atu ātmā eṉap peyar peṟum. parañāṉattaip peṟṟa anta ātmā iṟaivaṉuṭaṉ kalantu viṭukiṟatu.
māyai
ulakattiṉ nilaiyāmaiyē māyai eṉpatu. iṭam, kālam, kāraṇam mutaliyavai nam anupavattiṉ ātāramāy iruntum, meypōl tōṉṟip poyyāy maṟaikiṉṟaṉa. ivai, tammuḷ muraṇāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvulaka aṟivu ek kālattilum poruttamaṟṟa aṟivāka irukkiṉṟatu. namatu uḷḷuṇarvu mikumpōtu ivvulakam nam niṉaiviliruntu maṟaikiṉṟatu. cātāraṇa aṟivu (aparañāṉam) koṇṭu nām kāṇum poruḷkaḷ māyai eṉappaṭum. im maṟaiyum ulakattaiyum, ataṉ poruḷkaḷaiyum māyai eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟuvar. ulakattiṟku uṇmaittaṉmai illai eṉṟu maṉattai maṟaippatum māyait taṉmaitāṉ.
nilaiyaṟṟa ivvulakattaiyum (ulaka nikaḻccikaḷ) nilaiyuḷḷa ulakattaiyum (piramattōṭu kūṭiya ulakattaiyum) orē camayattil nām anupavikka muṭiyātu. in nilaiyaṟṟa ulakamum nilaiyāṉa ulakattāl nilaipeṟukiṉṟatu. āṉāl nam ciṟiya anupavamum, ivvuṇmaiyaik kāṭṭuvatillai. uḷḷuṇarvāltāṉ itai uṇara muṭiyum.
māyai eṉṟa iruḷāl, uyir, taṉ mēlāṉa aṟivu kuṉṟikkiṭakkiṟatu. mēlāṉa aṟivē inta iruḷai akaṟṟa muṭiyum. piramattaip paramāṉmā eṉṟum, uyirai jīvāṉmā eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iraṇṭum oṉṟē āyiṉum uṭampu māyai ivaṟṟāl kaṭṭuppaṭṭa jīvāṉmāvai nōkkap paramāṉmā vēṟupaṭṭatāka aṟiñar upacarittuk kūṟuvar.
iṟaivaṉai aṭaiyum vaḻikaḷ
uyir iṟaivaṉai aṭaiya mūṉṟu nilaikaḷaik kaṭattal vēṇṭum. avai, (1) kuruviṭam payiṟci peṟutal, (2) niṉaittup pārttal (Reflection), (3) vaḻipāṭu eṉpaṉa.
(1) mutalil kuruviṭam payiṟci peṟutal vēṇṭum; taṉakkum ulakap poruḷkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu eṉṉa eṉpatu pōṉṟa uṇmaikaḷaik kuruviṉ vāyilāka aṟitalē mutal nilai ākum.
(2) iṅṅaṉam kuruviṉiṭam aṟinta uṇmaikaḷait taṉ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil naṭaipeṟum nikaḻccikaḷaik koṇṭu aṟitalē iraṇṭām nilai.
(3) kuruviṉ vāyilāka iṟaivaṉaip paṟṟit tāṉ kēṭṭa uṇmaikaḷait taṉ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil anupavitta maṉitaṉ, taṉatu tiyāṉattiṉ vāyilākat teḷivāka aṟikiṉ ṟāṉ. inta nilaiyil avaṉ piramattai uṇaruvatōṭu, tāṉē piramamākavum ākiṉṟāṉ. inta nilaiyai aṭaipavaṉē cīvaṉ muktaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ivaṉai ellāp paṟṟukkaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭa maṉitaṉ eṉṟum kūṟalām. ivaṉatu ātmā uṭalaiviṭṭup pirintatum iṟaivaṉōṭu kalantu viṭukiṉṟatu.
maṉita vāḻkkai nīrōṭṭamuḷḷa āṟṟunīrk kumiḻiyai okkum; kumiḻiyum nīrtāṉ; ōṭum āṟṟilum atē nīrtāṉ irukkiṟatu. atē pōṉṟu cīvātmāvum paramātmāvum oṉṟē. ituvē atvaita tattuvattiṉ uyirnāṭi. inta atvaita tattuvam ēkāṉma vātam eṉṟum collappaṭum.
(4) viciṭṭāttuvaitam
itu irāmānucar upatēcitta camaya tattuvam. ivar ki.pi. 11- ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar; mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattiṉar.
āṉmāviṟkum uṭampiṟkum uḷḷa toṭarpu iraṇṭaṟṟa (atvaita) campantam. atupōlavē āṉmāvukkum iṟaivaṉukkum uḷḷa iraṇṭaṟṟa toṭarpum attuvaita toṭarpē. āṉāl āṉmāviṟkum iṟaivaṉukkum uḷḷa iraṇṭaṟṟa toṭarpu, āṉmāviṟkum uṭampiṟkum uḷḷa iraṇṭaṟṟa muṟaiyaiviṭa viciṭṭamāṉatu (ciṟantatu) paṟṟi viciṭṭāttuvaitam eṉappaṭṭatu. cīvāṉmā urimaiyaṟṟatu; paramāṉmā urimaiyuṭaiyatu; (vicēṭamāṉatu), itu paṟṟiya attuvaitam viciṭṭāttuvaitam eṉappaṭṭatu.
(1) ulakam, piramam ivai vevvēṟu āyiṉum oṉṟu eṉpatu atvaitam. ulakam, piramam ellām piramattiṉ uruvē. eṉavē, māyai eṉa oṉṟu illai eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvaitam.
(2) uyir piramattiṉ ellā amcaṅkaḷaiyum uṭaiyatu eṉpatu attuvaitam..  paṭaippu, ilakkumi nāyakattaṉmai eṉṟa iraṇṭum nīṅkalāka piramattiṟku uriya ellām uyirukkum uṇṭu eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvaitam.
(3) mutti eṉpatu pirama ñāṉattāl maṭṭumē uṇṭāvatu eṉpatu attuvaitam. muttikkup paktiyē aṭippaṭai; ñāṉa paktiyē ciṟappāṉatu eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvaitam.
(4) piramamum uyirum oṉṟē eṉpatu attuvaitam; piramam- uyir uṟavu, āṇṭāṉ- aṭimai uṟavu pōṉṟatu eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvaitam.
(5) piramamum uyirum oṉṟē eṉal attuvaitam; piramamum uyirum nāyaka- nāyaki uṟavu koṇṭavai eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvaitam.
(6) tattuvamaci- nītāṉ atu.
	akam prammāmi - nāṉē piramam.
ivai attuvaitak karuttukkaḷ.
		ōm namō nārāyaṇāya- eṭṭeḻuttu
		srīmat nārāyaṇa caraṇau caraṇam pirapatyē
		srīmat nārāyaṇāya nama:
ivai viciṭṭāttuvaita mantiraṅkaḷ.
uyir vīṭupēṟṟai aṭaiya iraṇṭu vaḻikaḷ uṇṭu. avai pirāpti eṉṟum pakti eṉṟum collappaṭum. pirāpti eṉpatu vaiṇavattil koṇṭa nampikkai vaḻiyāka vīṭupēṟu aṭaital; upaniṭataṅkaḷil kāṭṭappaṭum pakti neṟippaṭi vīṭaṭaiya mūṉṟu paṭikaḷaik kaṭattal vēṇṭum. avai karmayōkam, ñāṉayōkam, pattiyōkam eṉappaṭum.
(1) oruvaṉ taṉ kaṭamaikaḷaic ceytalum, taṉṉaiyē kaṭavuḷukku urimaiyākkutal evvāṟu eṉpatai uṇartalum karmayōkam eṉappaṭum. (2) karmayōkattil paḻakiyavaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya uṇmaiyāṉa nilaiyaiyum, nilaiyāmaiyākiya taṉmaiyaiyum, taṉakkum kaṭavuḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpaiyum uṇarvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam uṇarum muyaṟci ñāṉayōkam eṉappaṭum. (3) paktiyōkam eṉpatu kuruṭṭu nampikkaiyaṉṟu; uyarnta aṟiviṉai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa kaṭavuḷaip paṟṟiya tiyāṉattil uṇmaiyāṉa nampikkaiyum aṉpum koḷvatē paktiyōkam. im mūṉṟu nilaikaḷaiyum kaṭanta uyirē vīṭupēṟṟai aṭaiyum. ulakamum uyirum kaṭavuḷāl ākkappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē ulakam māyai aṉṟu. ulaka nilaiyāmai māttiram koṇṭu ulakam illai eṉak kūṟutal uṇmaiyaṉṟu.
5. tuvaitam
itaṉaik kaṇṭaṟintavar mātvāccāriyār. ivar teṉkaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil uṭuppi eṉṉum ciṟṟūril piṟantavar. ivar 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar (1199- 1278). ivar irāmānucaraip pōlavē taṉippaṭṭa kaṭavuḷiṭam nampikkai koṇṭavar. kaṭavuḷ vētaṅkaḷ tuṇaiyālum aṟiya muṭiyātavar. avar muṭivillāta aruḷaiyum āṟṟalaiyum koṇṭavar. taṉippaṭṭa uyir, ulakam, nēram ellām kaṭavuḷ aruḷāl iruppavai; avar aruḷ illāviṭil aḻipavai. ivai yāvum kaṭavuḷukkākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉmūlam avar aṭaivatu oṉṟumillai eṉpatu ivar karuttu.
ivar piramattil pala poruḷkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpar; iṟaivaṉum uyirum, iṟaivaṉum ulakamum, uyirum ulakamum, uyirum maṟṟōr uyirum, iṟaivaṉ taṉittum maṟṟavaikaḷuṭaṉ kalantum vāḻalām. uyirkaḷum ulakamum taṉittu iyaṅkum taṉmaiyaṟṟaṉa. iṟaivaṉ uyiraiyum poruḷkaḷaiyum āṇṭu varupavaṉē tavirap paṭaikkavō, aḻikkavō illai. iṟaivaṉ ivaṟṟiṟkut tuṇaiyākavē viḷaṅkukiṟāṉ. iṟaivaṉ eṅkum parantuḷḷāṉ. eṉavē iṟaivaṉōṭu uyirkaḷ cārntu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. aṇuvaip pōṉṟa uyirum, taṉatu aṟivut taṉmaiyāl ulakam muḻuvatum parantiruk kiṉṟatu. iraṇṭu uyirkaḷ orē taṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil ovvoṉṟiṉ anupavamum vēṟu paṭukiṉṟatu.
6. caiva cittāntam
cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷ
ki.pi. 400- 600 eṉṉum kāla ellaiyil ceyyappaṭṭatu tirumantiram. ataṉkaṇ mūvāyiram pāṭalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil caiva cittāntak karuttukkaḷ citaṟik kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. piṉ vanta appar, campantar mutaliyōr pāṭalkaḷilum caiva cittāntak karuttukkaḷ citaṟik kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ik karuttukkaḷ aṉaittaiyum muṟaippaṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟa caiva cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷ patiṉāṉku ākum. avai ki.pi. 12, 13, 14- ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ceyyappaṭṭavai. avai (1) tiruvuntiyār, (2) tirukkaḷiṟṟup paṭiyār, (3) civañāṉa pōtam, (4) civañāṉa cittiyār, (5) irupā- irupaḵtu, (6) uṇmai viḷakkam, (7) civappirakācam, (8) tiruvaruṭ payaṉ, (9) viṉā veṇpā, (10) pōṟṟip paḵṟoṭai, (11) koṭikkavi, (12) neñcu viṭutūtu, (13) uṇmaineṟi viḷakkam, (14) caṅkaṟpa nirākaraṇam eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ talaimaṇi, meykaṇṭār iyaṟṟiya civañāṉa pōtam. ip patiṉāṉku cāttiraṅkaḷiṉ cattupporuḷkaḷē caiva camaya cattuvam eṉpatu.
kaṭavuḷ uyirkaḷuṭaṉ attuvitamāka niṉṟu avaikaṭku viṉaip payaṉai ūṭṭuvār. uyirkaṭkup pācattoṭarpāl piṟappu iṟappu nikaḻum. uyir paruvuṭal, aṟikaruvikaḷ, nuṇṇuṭal, pirāṇavāyu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku vēṟāṉatu; tāṉē aṟivataṉṟi, uṇartta uṇarvatu; uṭaṟ kāraṇaṅkaḷ kūṭi iruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku vēṟāy niṉṟu aṟivatu; paṇṭaik kāla mutalē āṇavamalattāl kaṭṭuṇṭu kiṭappatu; puṟa - akakkaruvikaḷum uyirum, aracum amaiccum pōlvaṉa. ak karuvikaḷ uyirukkut tuṇai ceyvaṉa. ivai yāvum toḻiṟpaṭum kālam uyirukku naṉavu nilai eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ cila kuṟainta kālam uyirukkuk kaṉavu nilai; mēlum cila kuṟainta nilai uṟakka nilai eṉappaṭum. ivai yāvum nīṅki uyir taṉṉilaiyil niṉṟapōtu pēruṟakka nilai eṉappaṭum. uyiriṉ akaṅkāramum oṭuṅkiya nilai uyirppu aṭakkam eṉappaṭum.
karuvikaḷ aṟivaṟṟaṉa ātaliṉ tammaiyum aṟiyā; tammaic celuttum uyiriṉaiyum aṟiyā. avaipōla uyirkaḷum tammai yaṟiyā; tammaic celuttum iṟaivaṉaiyum aṟiyā.
cattu eṉṟum kēṭiṉṟi viḷaṅkum poruḷ. eṉavē iṟai oṉṟē avvilakkaṇattukku ilakkiyamākum. āyiṉum, caiva cittāntattil mupporuḷkaḷākiya iṟai, uyir, ulakam (pati, pacu, pācam) eṉpaṉa eṉṟum uḷḷa poruḷkaḷāka oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭavai. ākavē, ivai mūṉṟum cattēyākum. uyirum ulakamum vikāram aṭaivatāl acattātal peṟappaṭum. im mūṉṟiṉaiyum vēṟu pirittu aṟitaṟku ivai muṟaiyē civa- cittu, cata- cattu, caṭa- cattu eṉṟu pirikkappaṭum.
cārntataṉ vaṇṇamātal uyiriṉ ciṟappilakkaṇamākum. aḵtu acattākiya ulakattaiyum, cattākiya paramporuḷaiyum aṟiyavallatu; acattai viṭṭuc cattaip paṟṟakkūṭiyatu. uyirkaḷiṉ tavattāl iṟaivaṉ kuru vaṭivil vantu, pakkuvam aṭaivataṟku ñāṉattai uṇarttit taṉpāl avarkaḷaic cērppaṉ. ñāṉam peṟṟavar ainteḻuttu ōti ñāṉa nilaiyaik kāppar.
ivvāṟu ñāṉattaip pēṇum uyirkaḷ, iṟaivaṉ tammuṭaṉ ōṟṟittu niṟṟalāl pācam nīṅkappeṟum. iṟaivaṉ uyirkaḷukkut tuṇaiyāka niṉṟu civappēṟu allatu mutti nilaiyaik kāṭṭuvāṉ; ataṉaik kāṇumpaṭi utaviyum ceyvāṉ. cīvaṉ muktarkaḷ malanīkkak karuttuṭaiyavarāy, aṭiyār iṇakkam uṭaiyavarāyc civa vēṭattaiyum civaṉ kōvilaiyum vaḻipaṭum niyamamum uṭaiyavarāy niṟpar. civañāṉapōtam paṉṉiraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷ ituvēyākum.

12. ilakkiyak kalai
ilakkiyam
coṟkaḷaiyum vākkiyaṅkaḷaiyum uṇṭākkip pāṭavum pēcavum aṟinta makkaḷ, eḻuttu muṟaiyaik kaṇṭaṟinta piṉpu, tām atuvaraiyil vāḻaiyaṭi vāḻaiyāka aṟintu vaittirunta camutāyak karuttukaḷaiyum, camayak karuttukaḷaiyum pākkaḷil vaṭittaṉar. kālam cellac cella, aṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, moḻi vaḷam peṟṟatu. moḻiyil valla aṟiñarkaḷ makkaḷ paṭaitta ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum pēccu moḻiyaiyum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, moḻikku ilakkaṇam vakuttaṉar. piṉ vanta pulavarkaḷ anta ilakkaṇa amaitikkēṟpat tamiḻar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum nākarikattaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum kuṟikkum ceyyuṭkaḷaic ceytaṉar.
ceyyuḷ 
ceyyuḷ eṉpatu ēṟṟa cila coṟkaḷāl pala poruḷ kaḷai uḷḷaṭakkip poruttamāṉa muṟaiyil ceyyappaṭuvatu.
 	 cilvakai eḻuttil palvakaip poruḷaic
cevvaṉ āṭiyil ceṟittiṉitu viḷakkit
tiṭpa nuṭpam ciṟantaṉa cūttiram
eṉpatu naṉṉūl nūṟpā. ceyyuḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum ēṟattāḻa ituvē eṉṉalām.
	 palvakait tātuviṉ uyirkkuṭal pōlpala
collāl poruṭkiṭa ṉāka vuṇarviṉil
vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷ
eṉpatum kāṇka.
ilakkiyam eṉpatu makkaḷukkellām potuvākap payaṉpaṭum poruḷai uṭaiyatāy iruttal vēṇṭum; attuṭaṉ iṉiya vakaiyil collappaṭuvatāy iruttal vēṇṭum. coṟkaṭkuc ciṟanta iṉpam payattalaik kuṟik kōḷākap peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum; makkaḷuṭaiya vāḻkkai nikaḻccikaḷai eṭuttukkūṟum ceyyuṭkaḷō, nūlkaḷō, paṭippōratu uḷḷattaik koḷḷai koṇṭu, avaratu vāḻkkaiyaiyum vaḷappaṭutta vallaṉavātal vēṇṭum. ivaiyē ilakkiyam eṉappaṭum. (Literature is thus fundamentally an expression of Life through the medium of Language.- An Introduction to the study of Literature, Hudson, p. 11.) apimaṉyu kollappaṭṭa aṉṟiravu avaṉai niṉaittu avaṉ tantaiyāṉa aruccuṉaṉ pulampum pakutiyaip paṭippavar, tāmum kaṇṇīrviṭṭu aḻutalaip pārkkiṉṟōm. iṟappu ellōrkkum potu. iṟantatu kuṟittu pulamputalum ellōrkkum potu. eṉavē, ip pakuti aṉaivar uḷḷaṅkaḷaiyum īrkkiṉṟatu.
vāḻkkai nikaḻccikaḷai evarum kūṟalām. āyiṉ, avaṟṟaic cātāraṇa maṉitaṉ kūṟuvataṟkum ilakkaṇa ilakkiyam kaṟṟuḷḷa pulavaṉ kūṟuvataṟkum niṟainta vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. cātāraṇa maṉitaṉ kūṟuvatu elumpuk kūṭu pōṉṟatu. kaṟṟuvalla pulavaṉ kūṟuvatu uyiruḷḷa uṭal pōṉṟatu. nāppulamai vāynta pulavar kalaittiṟam poruntap poruḷai pukaṭṭuvatāl maṭṭumē nam uṇarvu kavarappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṉṉa poruḷai, iṉṉiṉṉa coṟkaḷāl, iṉṉiṉṉavāṟu, iṉṉiṉṉa kālattil colla vēṇṭum eṉpatai ap perumakkaḷ naṉkaṟivārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ceyyum ceyyuṭkaḷō nūlkaḷō kaṟpavarkaḷ uḷḷattaip piṇaikkavallavai. ittakaiya āṟṟalāl ilakkiyam kalai ākiṉṟatu.
ivvāṟu ciṟanta muṟaiyil ākkappeṟṟa nūlkaḷ talaiyāya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟu aṟiñarāl karutappeṟum. ap perumakkaḷatu ceyyuṭ ciṟappiṉai naṉku uṇarnta kampar perumāṉ piṉvarumāṟu pārāṭṭutal kāṇka:
	 puviyiṉuk kaṇiyāy āṉṟa poruḷ tantu pulattiṟ ṟāki
aviyakat tuṟaikaḷ tāṅki aintiṇai neṟiya ḷavic
caviyuṟat teḷintu taṇṇeṉ ṟoḻukkamum taḻuvic cāṉṟōr
kaviyeṉak kiṭanta kōtā variyiṉai vīrar kaṇṭār.
ilakkiyat tōṟṟam
oru camutāyattil ilakkiyam veḷippaṭuvaṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ yāvai?
oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar kalantu vāḻutal makkaḷ iyalpu. aṅṅaṉam makkaḷ oṉṟukūṭi vāḻntāltāṉ poruḷ uṇarcciyum aṟivu vaḷarcciyum uṇṭākum. makkaḷ tam uṇarccikaḷaiyum eṇṇaṅkaḷaiyum piṟarkku eṭuttuk kāṭṭa viḻaital iyalpākum; iṅṅaṉam eṭuttukkāṭṭi kēṭpavar avaṟṟai aṟiyumpoḻutu tammaip pōlavē uṇartalum eṇṇutalum ceytu ceytu makiḻvataip pārppatil iṉpam tōṉṟukiṟataṉṟō? ivvāṟu makkaḷ tammai veḷippaṭuttu vataṟku virumpum viruppa mikuti ilakkiyat tōṟṟattiṟku oru kāraṇamākum. im muṟaiyil eḻuntavaiyē akapporuḷ, puṟapporuḷ paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ eṉṉalām.
makkaḷ tam karuttukaḷaip piṟarukku eṭuttuk kūṟuvatōṭu, piṟar eṇṇaṅkaḷaiyum uṇarccikaḷaiyum aṟintu koḷvatil viruppam kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar; avaratu vāḻkkai nikaḻccikaḷaik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar allatu tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar; avaṟṟaip piṟarukku eṭuttukkūṟi avaṟṟiṉ paṭippiṉaiyai uṇarttukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟāl avarkkut tammai yoḻinta piṟariṭattuḷḷa paṟṟu naṉku pulaṉākum. intap paṟṟē cilappatikāram mutaliya peruṅkāviyaṅkaḷ eḻutak kāraṇamāy amaintatu. muṟṟum tuṟanta iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ kāraṇamākavē cilappatikārattai yāttaṉar eṉpatu,
	 araiciyal piḻaittōrkku aṟaṅkūṟ ṟāvatūum
uraicāl pattiṉikku uyarntōr ēttalum
ūḻviṉai yuṟuttuvan tūṭṭumeṉ patūum
cūḻviṉaic cilampu kāraṇa mākac
cilappati kāram eṉṉum peyarāl
nāṭṭutu miyāmōr pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷ
eṉṟu kūṟiyataik koṇṭu uṇaralām. cintāmaṇi, maṇimēkalai, periyapurāṇam, irāmāyaṇam mutaliya katai nūlkaḷ immuṟai paṟṟiyē ceyyappeṟṟavai.
makkaḷ, tuṉpuṟum ivvulaka vāḻkkaiyaik kaṇṭu kaṇṭu, iṉpam kāṇum muṟaiyil īṭupaṭutalum ilakkiyat tōṟṟattiṟku oru kāraṇamākum. itaṉāl tāṉ, illatu- iṉiyatu- nallatu- puṉainturai eṉap pulavarāl nāṭṭap peṟṟa oḻukkam nāṉkeṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. kōvai, kalampakam pōṉṟa puṉainturai nūlkaḷ immuṟai paṟṟi eḻuntavaiyēyākum.
uḷḷattait tiṟappataṟkum, atait tiruttuvataṟkum, purintukoṇṭu paṭippataic cerippataṟkum, tavaṟukaḷaip pōkkum vaṉmai uṇṭākkuvataṟkum, nallavaṟṟai mēṟ koḷvataṟkum, piṟarkku eṭutturaippataṟkum, piṟarai nalvaḻipaṭuttutaṟkum ilakkiyam payaṉpaṭal vēṇṭum. innilaiyiṟṟāṉ aḵtu ilakkiyam eṉappaṭum, eṉpatu kārṭiṉal niyūmaṉ eṉpavaratu karuttākum.
ivvāṟu ilakkiyam pala kāraṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ oḻuṅku, varaiyaṟai, aḻaku, payaṉ ākiyavai niṟaintuḷḷa ilakkiya nūlkaḷē paṭippōrkku iṉpa uṇarcciyai uṇṭākkum. avaiyē camutāyattiṉ aḻiyāta celvaṅkaḷākum. nam tamiḻ nūlkaḷuṭ pala ittakaiya aḻiyāta celvaṅkaḷākum. avaṟṟuḷ talaiciṟantavai caṅka nūlkaḷ; uḷḷatai uḷḷavāṟu uraippavai.
tokai nūlkaḷ
puṟanāṉūṟu, akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇai, kuṟuntokai, aiṅkuṟu nūṟu, patiṟṟup pattu, paripāṭal, kalittokai eṉpavai caṅkakālat tokai nūlkaḷ eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ paripāṭal eṉpatu, murukaṉ, tirumāl, vaiyai paṟṟip palar pāṭiya pākkaḷaik koṇṭatu. ovvoru pāṭṭiṟkum cantam uṇṭu. tirumāl, murukaṉ ākiya kaṭavuḷar alaṅkārac ciṟappum, ak kaṭavuḷar ceyta vīrac ceyalkaḷum, piṟavum aṭiyārkku aruḷum tiṟamum poruḷpotinta coṟkaḷāl pēcap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vaiyai yāṟṟil putuveḷḷam varutal, av veḷḷam vayalkaḷ mutaliya pala iṭaṅkaḷilum paravutal, avaṟṟāl uḻavar mutaliyōr makiḻvuṟutal, nakara makkaḷ putunīrāṭap pōtal, avartam nilaikaḷ, avarkaḷ nīrāṭutal, vaiyaip perukkiṟku vaṇakkam terivikkum muṟai, āṟṟaṅkaraiyil nikaḻum āṭal pāṭal nikaḻccikaḷ mutaliya vaiyai paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ aḻakoḻuka eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṇṭaip pulavar perumakkaḷ kaṭavuḷartam alaṅkāra vakaikaḷaiyum, potumakkaḷ pakti niṟainta uḷḷattōṭu kōvilukku varutalaiyum, ak kaṭavuḷarai vaḻipaṭutalaiyum, ivvāṟē vaiyaiyil putuveḷḷam varutalaiyum, potumakkaḷ makiḻcciyōṭu nīrāṭi āṭal pāṭalkaḷ nikaḻttu talaiyum tam viḻiyārak kaṇṭu kaṇṭu makiḻntavar; ak kāṭcikaḷai niṉaiviṟ koṇṭum, tām paṭitta nūlkaḷiṉ tuṇaiyaik koṇṭum, tamatu kaṟpaṉait tiṟaṉum pulamait tiṟaṉum pulappaṭa ip pāṭalkaḷaip pāṭiṉar. eṉavē, ip pāṭalkaḷ ilakkiyak kalaivaḷam ceṟintu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
akapporuḷ nūlkaḷ
akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇai, kuṟuntokai, aiṅkuṟunūṟu eṉpavai, illaṟam nallaṟamāka naṭattakkūṭiya uḷḷam oṉṟupaṭṭa talaimakkaḷatu aka vāḻvaic cittarittuk kūṟuvaṉa. ivvaka vāḻvu kuṟiñci, pālai, mullai, marutam, neytal eṉṉum aintu oḻukkaṅkaḷāka nikaḻkiṉṟatu eṉṟu, pulavarkaḷ ivaṟṟukku kuṟiñci mutaliya aintu tiṇaikaḷai oppiṭṭup pāṭiyuḷḷa tiṟam ciṟanta kalait tiṟaṉait taṉṉakattē koṇṭuḷḷatākum. ovvoru nilattiṟkum uriya maram ceṭi koṭikaḷ, paṟavaikaḷ, perum poḻutu, ciṟu poḻutu, paṟai, paṇ, toḻilkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai muṟaip paṭutti, avvoḻukkattai viḷakkum pāṭalkaḷ avvat tiṇaikkuriya mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷ, uripporuḷ eṉpavai poruntap pāṭiyuḷḷa tiṟaṉaik kalaittiṟaṉ eṉṟu collalām. caṅkap pulavar pāṭalkaḷaip paṭittup paṭittu aṟintu makiḻpavarē avartam kalaittiṟaṉai naṉku uṇara muṭiyum.
kalittokaiyil ciṟanta kalaimakkaḷatu akavoḻukkamum iḻintōr akavoḻukkamum kalantu pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iḻintōr paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷil iḻintōrkkuriya paṇpu kaḷaiyum, avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum uvamaikaḷaiyum coṟṟoṭar kaḷaiyum karuttukkaḷaiyum vaittup pāṭiyiruttal, caṅkap pulavartam kalaittiṟaṉaiyum, paranta ulaka aṟivaiyum naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟatu. ilakkiyak kalaikku ivvakap poruḷ nūlkaḷ ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu eṉṉalām.
tamiḻaṉ, pōrai veṭci mutaliya pala tiṇaikaḷākap pirittāṉ; ovvoru tiṇaikkum pala tuṟaikaḷai amaittāṉ. ovvoru tuṟaiyum pōr muyaṟciyiṉ oru paṭiyākum. iṅṅaṉam pōr muṟaikaḷukku ilakkaṇam vakutta tamiḻaṉ, pōrukku appāṟpaṭṭa ceyalkaḷukkum pala pakutikaḷai vakuttāṉ. ivai aṉaittaiyum tolkāppiyam- puṟattiṇai iyalilum, puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālai yilum virivākak kāṇalām. it tiṇaikaḷukkum, tuṟai kaḷukkum uriya pāṭalkaḷaip puṟanāṉūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu eṉṉum iraṇṭu nūlkaḷilum paṭittu makiḻalām. ovvoru pāṭṭiṉ aṭiyilum, iḵtu iṉṉa tiṇai- iṉṉa tuṟai eṉṟu paṇṭaip pulavarkaḷ eḻuti vaittuḷḷārkaḷ. tiṇaiyum tuṟaiyum paṟṟip pēcum ilakkaṇattiṟkuriya eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ovvoru pāṭalum amaintiruppatu paṭittu uṇarntu makiḻaṟ kuriyatu. mikac ciṟanta kalaiyuṇarvu uṭaiya pulavar perumakkaḷāltām ittakaiya pāṭalkaḷaip pāṭutal iyalum.
tirukkuṟaḷ 
tirukkuṟaḷ aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam eṉṟa mūṉṟu poruḷkaḷaiyum virittup pēcuvatu; iṭaiyiṭaiyē vīṭu pēṟṟaiyum kuṟippatu; vāḻkkaikkut tēvaiyāṉa ovvoru viḻumiya karuttaiyum kuṟaṭpāvil terivippatu. kuṟaṭpā iraṇṭu aṭikaḷaik koṇṭatu; ēḻu cīrkaḷai uṭaiyatu. oru ciṟanta karuttai ēḻu cīrkaḷāl oṉṟē mukkāl aṭiyil amaittal eḷitāṉa ceyalā? oru kuṟaḷil oru collai nīkki nām vēṟoru collai amaittāl, ak kuṟaḷiṉ poruḷ amaitiyum collamaitiyum keṭṭu viṭutal kāṇkiṟōm. iṅṅaṉam piṟar kai vaikka muṭiyātapaṭi, ciṟanta coṟkaḷai amaittu avaṟṟāl viḻumiya karuttai viḷakkuvatu ciṟanta kalaiyāṟṟal aṉṟō? ittakaiya kalai āṟṟalait tirukkuṟaḷil kaṇṭu kaṇṭu cuvaikkalām.
cilappatikāram
cilappatikāram tamiḻk kāviyaṅkaḷuḷ talaiciṟantatu eṉṟu aṉaivarum pōṟṟutaṟku, ataṉkaṇ amaintuḷḷa kalai āṟṟalē ciṟanta kāraṇamākum. atu maṉita vāḻvaic cittarittuk kūṟum pērilakkiyam; maṉitaṉuṭaiya nalliyalpu kaḷaiyum, tīya iyalpukaḷaiyum paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭuvatu; oṉpatu cuvaikaḷaiyum tiṟampaṭa eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu; tamiḻakattiṉ aivakai nilaṅkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ iyalpu kaḷaiyum virittuk kūṟuvatu; moḻippaṟṟu - iṉap paṟṟu- nāṭṭuppaṟṟu eṉpavaṟṟai uyir nāṭiyākak koṇṭu iruppatu; viruppu veṟuppu iṉṟic cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar mūvaraiyum oṉṟu pōlavē pārāṭṭuvatu. ittakaiya paṇpukaḷōṭu viḻumiya coṟkaḷāl yākkappeṟṟa akavaṟ pākkaḷaiyum veṇpākkaḷaiyum taṉṉakattē peṟṟiruppatu; pala karuttukkaḷ alaimōtum uḷḷattiṟkuc ciṟanta aṟivuraiyai iṟutiyil aḷittu niṟpatu. ittakaiya uyarnta paṇpukaḷāl cilappatikāram eṉṉum kāviyam talaiciṟanta ilakkiyak kalai nūlāyk kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.
muṭivurai
ivvāṟē piṉtōṉṟiya nūlkaḷāṉa cintāmaṇi, periya purāṇam, kampa rāmāyaṇam eṉpaṉa ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṉalām. caivat tirumuṟaikaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, āḻvār aruṭpāṭalkaḷum camaya ilakkiyam; patiṉāṉku cāttiraṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa tattuva ilakkiyam. paraṇi, kalampakam, ulā, antāti, piḷḷaittamiḻ, kuṟam mutaliya ciṟu nūlkaḷ pulavartam ilakkiyak kalaiyāṟṟalai naṉmuṟaiyil veḷip paṭuttuvaṉavākum. iṅṅaṉamē ilakkiyap paṇpukaḷ amainta ik kālak katai nūlkaḷ kavitaikaḷ, nāṭaka nūlkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum ilakkiya araṅkil iṭampeṟum. ivaṟṟaiyellām ilakkiyak kalai uṇarvōṭu paṭittu iṉpuṟutalē tamiḻar kaṭamaiyākum.
